FORGE LICENSES AND THIRD-PARTY NOTICES

Scope: the five-target union selected by the reviewed scoped Cargo tree command.
Development-only dependency edges are excluded. Each native release-build independently
requires its compiler-artifact package set to equal this scoped graph before staging.
Registry package identity comes from Cargo.lock, and each fetched .crate
archive is required to match its Cargo.lock SHA-256. Legal text is separately
read from the current Cargo-unpacked source tree; the archive check is not
claimed to prove those unpacked bytes, which are fixed by reviewed per-file SHA-256.
Every detected LICENSE, LICENCE, COPYING, NOTICE, COPYRIGHT, and UNLICENSE file is
reproduced per package.

Packages: 96 total (6 workspace, 90 registry).
